class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eFalse\u003c/span\u003e,\n    ),\n)\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eX.509 mode defaults to \u003ccode\u003ehttps://mtls.api.openai.com/v1\u003c/code\u003e when neither \u003ccode\u003ebase_url\u003c/code\u003e\nnor \u003ccode\u003eOPENAI_BASE_URL\u003c/code\u003e is set. The same configured HTTP client presents its\ncertificate to the fixed mTLS token-exchange endpoint and to the API. Tokens\nare exchanged lazily, cached, and refreshed automatically. Certificate files,\nprivate keys, passwords, server trust, proxies, and rotation remain application\nand transport concerns.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eX.509 API requests require HTTPS and must stay on the configured API origin.\nThe effective HTTP Host authority must match that origin.\nProvider API-key and proxy-only headers cannot be sent to the API alongside\nX.509 authentication.\nToken exchanges do not inherit API request hooks, authentication, or cookies.\nIdentity settings are captured when the client is constructed; create a new\nclient to change the identity. Azure clients do not support X.509 workload\nidentity.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor asynchronous requests, use \u003ccode\u003eAsyncOpenAI\u003c/code\u003e with\n\u003ccode\u003eDefaultAsyncHttpx2Client\u003c/code\u003e. data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe Realtime API enables you to build low-latency, multi-modal conversational experiences. It currently supports text and audio as both input and output, as well as \u003ca href=\"https://platform.openai.com/docs/guides/function-calling\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003efunction calling\u003c/a\u003e through a WebSocket connection.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUnder the hood the SDK uses the \u003ca href=\"https://websockets.readthedocs.io/en/stable/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ewebsockets\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e library to manage connections.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe Realtime API works through a combination of client-sent events and server-sent events. Clients can send events to do things like update session configuration or send text and audio inputs. Server events confirm when audio responses have completed, or when a text response from the model has been received. A full event reference can be found \u003ca href=\"https://platform.openai.com/docs/api-reference/realtime-client-events\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehere\u003c/a\u003e and a guide can be found \u003ca href=\"https://platform.openai.com/docs/guides/realtime\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehere\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBasic text based example:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"import asyncio\nfrom openai import AsyncOpenAI\n\nasync def main():\n    client = AsyncOpenAI()\n\n    async with client.realtime.connect(model=\u0026quot;gpt-realtime-2\u0026quot;) as connection:\n        await connection.session.update(\n            session={\u0026quot;type\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;realtime\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;output_modalities\u0026quot;: [\u0026quot;text\u0026quot;]}\n        )\n\n        await connection.conversation.item.create(\n            item={\n                \u0026quot;type\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;message\u0026quot;,\n                \u0026quot;role\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;user\u0026quot;,\n                \u0026quot;content\u0026quot;: [{\u0026quot;type\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;input_text\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;text\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;Say hello!\u0026quot;}],\n            }\n        )\n        await connection.response.create()\n\n        async for event in connection:\n            if event.type == \u0026quot;response.output_text.delta\u0026quot;:\n                print(event.delta, flush=True, end=\u0026quot;\u0026quot;)\n\n            elif event.type == \u0026quot;response.output_text.done\u0026quot;:\n                print()\n\n            elif event.type == \u0026quot;response.done\u0026quot;:\n                break\n\nasyncio.run(main())\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003easyncio\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eclient\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003erealtime\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003econnect\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eas\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003econnection\u003c/span\u003e:\n        \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eawait\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003econnection\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003esession\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eupdate\u003c/span\u003e(\n            \u003cspan 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor most use cases, you will likely want to verify the webhook and parse the payload at the same time. To achieve this, we provide the method \u003ccode\u003eclient.webhooks.unwrap()\u003c/code\u003e, which parses a webhook request and verifies that it was sent by OpenAI. This method will raise an error if the signature is invalid.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNote that the \u003ccode\u003ebody\u003c/code\u003e parameter must be the raw JSON string sent from the server (do not parse it first). The \u003ccode\u003e.unwrap()\u003c/code\u003e method will parse this JSON for you into an event object after verifying the webhook was sent from OpenAI.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"from openai import OpenAI\nfrom flask import Flask, request\n\napp = Flask(__name__)\nclient = OpenAI()  # OPENAI_WEBHOOK_SECRET environment variable is used by default\n\n\n@app.route(\u0026quot;/webhook\u0026quot;, methods=[\u0026quot;POST\u0026quot;])\ndef webhook():\n    request_body = request.get_data(as_text=True)\n\n    try:\n        event = client.webhooks.unwrap(request_body, request.headers)\n\n        if event.type == \u0026quot;response.completed\u0026quot;:\n            print(\u0026quot;Response completed:\u0026quot;, event.data)\n        elif event.type == \u0026quot;response.failed\u0026quot;:\n            print(\u0026quot;Response failed:\u0026quot;, event.data)\n        else:\n            print(\u0026quot;Unhandled event type:\u0026quot;, event.type)\n\n        return \u0026quot;ok\u0026quot;\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(\u0026quot;Invalid signature:\u0026quot;, e)\n        return \u0026quot;Invalid signature\u0026quot;, 400\n\n\nif __name__ == \u0026quot;__main__\u0026quot;:\n    app.run(port=8000)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eopenai\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan HTTPX2\u003c/a\u003e examples.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe certificate-bearing HTTP client is transport-wide. For API-key mTLS,\ndedicate it to the selected API origin; X.509 workload identity also uses the\nfixed OpenAI mTLS token-exchange origin. Do not reuse the client for unrelated\nservices or pass it through \u003ccode\u003ewith_options()\u003c/code\u003e with a different \u003ccode\u003ebase_url\u003c/code\u003e.\nIf redirects are required for API-key mTLS, add an\nHTTPX2 request hook that rejects requests whose scheme, host, or port differs\nfrom the configured mTLS origin before enabling \u003ccode\u003efollow_redirects\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSSLContext.load_cert_chain()\u003c/code\u003e raises during setup for unreadable or malformed\nfiles and for a private key that does not match the leaf certificate. Certificate\nexpiry, key usage, extended key usage, SAN, and trust policy remain TLS server\ndecisions. OpenAI does not fetch missing intermediates through AIA, so provide a\ncomplete, leaf-first client-chain PEM. Intermediate-chain support is currently\nenabled by request. Until it is enabled for your organization, use a client leaf\ncertificate directly signed by the uploaded CA.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor certificate rotation, build a new \u003ccode\u003eSSLContext\u003c/code\u003e, HTTP client, and \u003ccode\u003eOpenAI\u003c/code\u003e or\n\u003ccode\u003eAsyncOpenAI\u003c/code\u003e client. This creates a fresh connection pool; close the old SDK\nclient after its in-flight requests finish. Do not assume existing TLS\nconnections will renegotiate.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis recipe applies to ordinary API-key HTTP traffic. AWS controls which endpoints and features are supported; unsupported calls surface the provider's normal HTTP errors through the SDK.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe default AWS credential chain supports environment credentials, shared credentials and config files, named profiles, SSO and assume-role profiles, and workload credentials such as ECS, EKS, and EC2 metadata. Low-level one-shot request streams must be buffered before sending, or sent with bearer authentication and retries disabled.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBearer tokens remain available as a compatibility or manual authentication mode. class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">OpenAI Python API library</h1><a id="user-content-openai-python-api-library" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OpenAI Python API library" href="#openai-python-api-library"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto"><a href="https://pypi.org/project/openai/" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/8604f02a8c199de45f717e017aad786238bf1115870db170c2d54c09a8d9bc4b/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f707970692f762f6f70656e61692e7376673f6c6162656c3d7079706925323028737461626c6529" alt="PyPI version" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/openai.svg?label=pypi%20(stable)" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">The OpenAI Python library provides convenient access to the OpenAI REST API from any Python 3.10+
application. The library includes type definitions for all request params and response fields,
and offers both synchronous and asynchronous clients powered by <a href="https://httpx2.pydantic.dev/" rel="nofollow">HTTPX2</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">It is generated from our <a href="https://github.com/openai/openai-openapi">OpenAPI specification</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Documentation</h2><a id="user-content-documentation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Documentation" href="#documentation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The REST API documentation can be found on <a href="https://platform.openai.com/docs/api-reference" rel="nofollow">platform.openai.com</a>. The full API of this library can be found in <a href="/openai/openai-python/blob/main/api.md">api.md</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h2><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# install from PyPI
pip install openai"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> install from PyPI</span>
pip install openai</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Usage</h2><a id="user-content-usage" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Usage" href="#usage"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The full API of this library can be found in <a href="/openai/openai-python/blob/main/api.md">api.md</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">The primary API for interacting with OpenAI models is the <a href="https://platform.openai.com/docs/api-reference/responses" rel="nofollow">Responses API</a>. You can generate text from the model with the code below.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import os
from openai import OpenAI

client = OpenAI(
    # This is the default and can be omitted
    api_key=os.environ.get(&quot;OPENAI_API_KEY&quot;),
)

response = client.responses.create(
    model=&quot;gpt-5.5&quot;,
    instructions=&quot;You are a coding assistant that talks like a pirate.&quot;,
    input=&quot;How do I check if a Python object is an instance of a class?&quot;,
)

print(response.output_text)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">os</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-c"># This is the default and can be omitted</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">api_key</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_API_KEY"</span>),
)

<span class="pl-s1">response</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">responses</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-5.5"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">instructions</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"You are a coding assistant that talks like a pirate."</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">input</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"How do I check if a Python object is an instance of a class?"</span>,
)

<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">output_text</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The previous standard (supported indefinitely) for generating text is the <a href="https://platform.openai.com/docs/api-reference/chat" rel="nofollow">Chat Completions API</a>. You can use that API to generate text from the model with the code below.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI

client = OpenAI()

completion = client.chat.completions.create(
    model=&quot;gpt-5.5&quot;,
    messages=[
        {&quot;role&quot;: &quot;developer&quot;, &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Talk like a pirate.&quot;},
        {
            &quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;,
            &quot;content&quot;: &quot;How do I check if a Python object is an instance of a class?&quot;,
        },
    ],
)

print(completion.choices[0].message.content)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>()

<span class="pl-s1">completion</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">chat</span>.<span class="pl-c1">completions</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-5.5"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">messages</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[
        {<span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"developer"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Talk like a pirate."</span>},
        {
            <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>,
            <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"How do I check if a Python object is an instance of a class?"</span>,
        },
    ],
)

<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">completion</span>.<span class="pl-c1">choices</span>[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>].<span class="pl-c1">message</span>.<span class="pl-c1">content</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">While you can provide an <code>api_key</code> keyword argument,
we recommend using <a href="https://pypi.org/project/python-dotenv/" rel="nofollow">python-dotenv</a>
to add <code>OPENAI_API_KEY="My API Key"</code> to your <code>.env</code> file
so that your API key is not stored in source control.
<a href="https://platform.openai.com/settings/organization/api-keys" rel="nofollow">Get an API key here</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Workload Identity Authentication</h3><a id="user-content-workload-identity-authentication" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Workload Identity Authentication" href="#workload-identity-authentication"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For secure, automated environments like cloud-managed Kubernetes, Azure, and Google Cloud Platform, you can use workload identity authentication with short-lived tokens from cloud identity providers instead of long-lived API keys.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Kubernetes (service account tokens)</h4><a id="user-content-kubernetes-service-account-tokens" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Kubernetes (service account tokens)" href="#kubernetes-service-account-tokens"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Custom subject token provider</h4><a id="user-content-custom-subject-token-provider" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Custom subject token provider" href="#custom-subject-token-provider"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI

<p dir="auto">You can also customize the token refresh buffer (default is 1200 seconds (20 minutes) before expiration):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">X.509 workload identity (mutual TLS)</h4><a id="user-content-x509-workload-identity-mutual-tls" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: X.509 workload identity (mutual TLS)" href="#x509-workload-identity-mutual-tls"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For X.509 workload identity federation, configure the client certificate and
server trust on an HTTPX2 client, then pass only the identity-provider and
service-account IDs to the SDK:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import os
import ssl

from openai import OpenAI, DefaultHttpx2Client
from openai.auth import x509_workload_identity

tls_context = ssl.create_default_context(
    cafile=os.getenv(&quot;OPENAI_MTLS_CA_BUNDLE&quot;),
)
tls_context.load_cert_chain(
    certfile=os.environ[&quot;OPENAI_MTLS_CERTIFICATE_CHAIN&quot;],
    keyfile=os.environ[&quot;OPENAI_MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY&quot;],
    password=os.getenv(&quot;OPENAI_MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD&quot;),
)

client = OpenAI(
    workload_identity=x509_workload_identity(
        identity_provider_id=os.environ[&quot;OPENAI_IDENTITY_PROVIDER_ID&quot;],
        service_account_id=os.environ[&quot;OPENAI_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_ID&quot;],
        # refresh_buffer_seconds=120.0,
    ),
    http_client=DefaultHttpx2Client(
        verify=tls_context,
        follow_redirects=False,
    ),
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">os</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">ssl</span>

<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>, <span class="pl-v">DefaultHttpx2Client</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span>.<span class="pl-s1">auth</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">x509_workload_identity</span>

<span class="pl-s1">tls_context</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">ssl</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create_default_context</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">cafile</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">getenv</span>(<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_MTLS_CA_BUNDLE"</span>),
)
<span class="pl-s1">tls_context</span>.<span class="pl-c1">load_cert_chain</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">certfile</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>[<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_MTLS_CERTIFICATE_CHAIN"</span>],
    <span class="pl-s1">keyfile</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>[<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY"</span>],
    <span class="pl-s1">password</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">getenv</span>(<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD"</span>),
)

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">workload_identity</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">x509_workload_identity</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">identity_provider_id</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>[<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_IDENTITY_PROVIDER_ID"</span>],
        <span class="pl-s1">service_account_id</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>[<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_ID"</span>],
        <span class="pl-c"># refresh_buffer_seconds=120.0,</span>
    ),
    <span class="pl-s1">http_client</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">DefaultHttpx2Client</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">verify</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">tls_context</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">follow_redirects</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">False</span>,
    ),
)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">X.509 mode defaults to <code>https://mtls.api.openai.com/v1</code> when neither <code>base_url</code>
nor <code>OPENAI_BASE_URL</code> is set. The same configured HTTP client presents its
certificate to the fixed mTLS token-exchange endpoint and to the API. Tokens
are exchanged lazily, cached, and refreshed automatically. Certificate files,
private keys, passwords, server trust, proxies, and rotation remain application
and transport concerns.</p>
<p dir="auto">X.509 API requests require HTTPS and must stay on the configured API origin.
The effective HTTP Host authority must match that origin.
Provider API-key and proxy-only headers cannot be sent to the API alongside
X.509 authentication.
Token exchanges do not inherit API request hooks, authentication, or cookies.
Identity settings are captured when the client is constructed; create a new
client to change the identity. Azure clients do not support X.509 workload
identity.</p>
<p dir="auto">For asynchronous requests, use <code>AsyncOpenAI</code> with
<code>DefaultAsyncHttpx2Client</code>. See the complete <a href="/openai/openai-python/blob/main/examples/x509_workload_identity.py">sync rollout-toggle
example</a> and <a href="/openai/openai-python/blob/main/examples/x509_workload_identity_async.py">async rollout-toggle
example</a>, which select API-key or
X.509 authentication with the application-owned <code>OPENAI_AUTH_MODE</code>
environment variable. X.509 workload identity currently supports HTTP APIs;
Realtime and WebSockets are not included.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Vision</h3><a id="user-content-vision" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Vision" href="#vision"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">With an image URL:</p>

<p dir="auto">With the image as a base64 encoded string:</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Async usage</h2><a id="user-content-async-usage" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Async usage" href="#async-usage"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Simply import <code>AsyncOpenAI</code> instead of <code>OpenAI</code> and use <code>await</code> with each API call:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import os
import asyncio
from openai import AsyncOpenAI

client = AsyncOpenAI(
    # This is the default and can be omitted
    api_key=os.environ.get(&quot;OPENAI_API_KEY&quot;),
)


async def main() -&gt; None:
    response = await client.responses.create(
        model=&quot;gpt-5.5&quot;, input=&quot;Explain disestablishmentarianism to a smart five year old.&quot;
    )
    print(response.output_text)


asyncio.run(main())"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">os</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">AsyncOpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">AsyncOpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-c"># This is the default and can be omitted</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">api_key</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_API_KEY"</span>),
)


<span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">main</span>() <span class="pl-c1">-&gt;</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span>:
    <span class="pl-s1">response</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">responses</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-5.5"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">input</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"Explain disestablishmentarianism to a smart five year old."</span>
    )
    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">output_text</span>)


<span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">run</span>(<span class="pl-en">main</span>())</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Functionality between the synchronous and asynchronous clients is otherwise identical.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">With aiohttp</h3><a id="user-content-with-aiohttp" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: With aiohttp" href="#with-aiohttp"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">By default, the async client uses HTTPX2. For improved concurrency performance, you may also use <code>aiohttp</code> as the HTTPX2 transport.</p>
<p dir="auto">You can enable this by installing <code>aiohttp</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# install from PyPI
pip install openai[aiohttp]"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> install from PyPI</span>
pip install openai[aiohttp]</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Then you can enable it by instantiating the client with <code>http_client=DefaultAioHttpClient()</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import os
import asyncio
from openai import DefaultAioHttpClient
from openai import AsyncOpenAI


async def main() -&gt; None:
    async with AsyncOpenAI(
        api_key=os.environ.get(&quot;OPENAI_API_KEY&quot;),  # This is the default and can be omitted
        http_client=DefaultAioHttpClient(),
    ) as client:
        chat_completion = await client.chat.completions.create(
            messages=[
                {
                    &quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;,
                    &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Say this is a test&quot;,
                }
            ],
            model=&quot;gpt-5.5&quot;,
        )


asyncio.run(main())"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">os</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">DefaultAioHttpClient</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">AsyncOpenAI</span>


<span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">main</span>() <span class="pl-c1">-&gt;</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span>:
    <span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">with</span> <span class="pl-en">AsyncOpenAI</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">api_key</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_API_KEY"</span>),  <span class="pl-c"># This is the default and can be omitted</span>
        <span class="pl-s1">http_client</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">DefaultAioHttpClient</span>(),
    ) <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>:
        <span class="pl-s1">chat_completion</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">chat</span>.<span class="pl-c1">completions</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
            <span class="pl-s1">messages</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[
                {
                    <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>,
                    <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Say this is a test"</span>,

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">HTTPX2 migration</h3><a id="user-content-httpx2-migration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: HTTPX2 migration" href="#httpx2-migration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">HTTPX2 is the default HTTP client. If you configure a custom HTTP client, transport, timeout, authentication handler, event hook, or request mock, see the <a href="/openai/openai-python/blob/main/httpx2.md">HTTPX2 migration guide</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Streaming responses</h2><a id="user-content-streaming-responses" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Streaming responses" href="#streaming-responses"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We provide support for streaming responses using Server Side Events (SSE).</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI

client = OpenAI()

stream = client.responses.create(
    model=&quot;gpt-5.5&quot;,
    input=&quot;Write a one-sentence bedtime story about a unicorn.&quot;,
    stream=True,
)

for event in stream:
    print(event)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>()

<span class="pl-s1">stream</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">responses</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-5.5"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">input</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"Write a one-sentence bedtime story about a unicorn."</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">stream</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>,
)

<span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">event</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-s1">stream</span>:
    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">event</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The async client uses the exact same interface.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import asyncio
from openai import AsyncOpenAI

client = AsyncOpenAI()


async def main():
    stream = await client.responses.create(
        model=&quot;gpt-5.5&quot;,
        input=&quot;Write a one-sentence bedtime story about a unicorn.&quot;,
        stream=True,
    )

    async for event in stream:
        print(event)


asyncio.run(main())"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">AsyncOpenAI</span>()


<span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">main</span>():
    <span class="pl-s1">stream</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">responses</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(

        <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">event</span>)

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Realtime API</h2><a id="user-content-realtime-api" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Realtime API" href="#realtime-api"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The Realtime API enables you to build low-latency, multi-modal conversational experiences. It currently supports text and audio as both input and output, as well as <a href="https://platform.openai.com/docs/guides/function-calling" rel="nofollow">function calling</a> through a WebSocket connection.</p>
<p dir="auto">Under the hood the SDK uses the <a href="https://websockets.readthedocs.io/en/stable/" rel="nofollow"><code>websockets</code></a> library to manage connections.</p>
<p dir="auto">The Realtime API works through a combination of client-sent events and server-sent events. Clients can send events to do things like update session configuration or send text and audio inputs. Server events confirm when audio responses have completed, or when a text response from the model has been received. A full event reference can be found <a href="https://platform.openai.com/docs/api-reference/realtime-client-events" rel="nofollow">here</a> and a guide can be found <a href="https://platform.openai.com/docs/guides/realtime" rel="nofollow">here</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Basic text based example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import asyncio
from openai import AsyncOpenAI

async def main():
    client = AsyncOpenAI()

    async with client.realtime.connect(model=&quot;gpt-realtime-2&quot;) as connection:
        await connection.session.update(
            session={&quot;type&quot;: &quot;realtime&quot;, &quot;output_modalities&quot;: [&quot;text&quot;]}
        )

        await connection.conversation.item.create(
            item={
                &quot;type&quot;: &quot;message&quot;,
                &quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;,
                &quot;content&quot;: [{&quot;type&quot;: &quot;input_text&quot;, &quot;text&quot;: &quot;Say hello!&quot;}],
            }
        )
        await connection.response.create()

        async for event in connection:
            if event.type == &quot;response.output_text.delta&quot;:
                print(event.delta, flush=True, end=&quot;&quot;)

            elif event.type == &quot;response.output_text.done&quot;:
                print()

            elif event.type == &quot;response.done&quot;:
                break

asyncio.run(main())"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">AsyncOpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">main</span>():
    <span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">AsyncOpenAI</span>()

    <span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">with</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">realtime</span>.<span class="pl-c1">connect</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-realtime-2"</span>) <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">connection</span>:
        <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">connection</span>.<span class="pl-c1">session</span>.<span class="pl-c1">update</span>(
            <span class="pl-s1">session</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>{<span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"realtime"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"output_modalities"</span>: [<span class="pl-s">"text"</span>]}
        )

        <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">connection</span>.<span class="pl-c1">conversation</span>.<span class="pl-c1">item</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
            <span class="pl-s1">item</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>{
                <span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"message"</span>,
                <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>,
                <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: [{<span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"input_text"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"text"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Say hello!"</span>}],
            }
        )
        <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">connection</span>.<span class="pl-c1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>()

        <span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">event</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-s1">connection</span>:
            <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s">"response.output_text.delta"</span>:
                <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">delta</span>, <span class="pl-s1">flush</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>, <span class="pl-s1">end</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">""</span>)

            <span class="pl-k">elif</span> <span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s">"response.output_text.done"</span>:
                <span class="pl-en">print</span>()

            <span class="pl-k">elif</span> <span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s">"response.done"</span>:
                <span class="pl-k">break</span>

<span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">run</span>(<span class="pl-en">main</span>())</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">However the real magic of the Realtime API is handling audio inputs / outputs, see this example <a href="https://github.com/openai/openai-python/blob/main/examples/realtime/push_to_talk_app.py">TUI script</a> for a fully fledged example.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Realtime error handling</h3><a id="user-content-realtime-error-handling" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Realtime error handling" href="#realtime-error-handling"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Whenever an error occurs, the Realtime API will send an <a href="https://platform.openai.com/docs/guides/realtime-model-capabilities#error-handling" rel="nofollow"><code>error</code> event</a> and the connection will stay open and remain usable. This means you need to handle it yourself, as <em>no errors are raised directly</em> by the SDK when an <code>error</code> event comes in.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="client = AsyncOpenAI()

async with client.realtime.connect(model=&quot;gpt-realtime-2&quot;) as connection:
    ...
    async for event in connection:
        if event.type == 'error':
            print(event.error.type)
            print(event.error.code)
            print(event.error.event_id)
            print(event.error.message)"><pre><span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">AsyncOpenAI</span>()

<span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">with</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">realtime</span>.<span class="pl-c1">connect</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-realtime-2"</span>) <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">connection</span>:
    ...
    <span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">event</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-s1">connection</span>:
        <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s">'error'</span>:
            <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">error</span>.<span class="pl-c1">type</span>)
            <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">error</span>.<span class="pl-c1">code</span>)
            <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">error</span>.<span class="pl-c1">event_id</span>)
            <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">error</span>.<span class="pl-c1">message</span>)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Using types</h2><a id="user-content-using-types" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Using types" href="#using-types"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Nested request parameters are <a href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/typing.html#typing.TypedDict" rel="nofollow">TypedDicts</a>. Responses are <a href="https://docs.pydantic.dev" rel="nofollow">Pydantic models</a> which also provide helper methods for things like:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Serializing back into JSON, <code>model.to_json()</code></li>
<li>Converting to a dictionary, <code>model.to_dict()</code></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Typed requests and responses provide autocomplete and documentation within your editor. If you would like to see type errors in VS Code to help catch bugs earlier, set <code>python.analysis.typeCheckingMode</code> to <code>basic</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Pagination</h2><a id="user-content-pagination" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Pagination" href="#pagination"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">List methods in the OpenAI API are paginated.</p>
<p dir="auto">This library provides auto-paginating iterators with each list response, so you do not have to request successive pages manually:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI

client = OpenAI()

all_jobs = []
# Automatically fetches more pages as needed.
for job in client.fine_tuning.jobs.list(
    limit=20,
):
    # Do something with job here
    all_jobs.append(job)
print(all_jobs)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>()

<span class="pl-s1">all_jobs</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> []
<span class="pl-c"># Automatically fetches more pages as needed.</span>
<span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">job</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">fine_tuning</span>.<span class="pl-c1">jobs</span>.<span class="pl-c1">list</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">limit</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">20</span>,
):
    <span class="pl-c"># Do something with job here</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">all_jobs</span>.<span class="pl-c1">append</span>(<span class="pl-s1">job</span>)
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">all_jobs</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Or, asynchronously:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import asyncio
from openai import AsyncOpenAI

client = AsyncOpenAI()


async def main() -&gt; None:
    all_jobs = []
    # Iterate through items across all pages, issuing requests as needed.
    async for job in client.fine_tuning.jobs.list(
        limit=20,
    ):
        all_jobs.append(job)
    print(all_jobs)


asyncio.run(main())"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>

<p dir="auto">Alternatively, you can use the <code>.has_next_page()</code>, <code>.next_page_info()</code>, or <code>.get_next_page()</code> methods for more granular control working with pages:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="first_page = await client.fine_tuning.jobs.list(
    limit=20,
)
if first_page.has_next_page():
    print(f&quot;will fetch next page using these details: {first_page.next_page_info()}&quot;)
    next_page = await first_page.get_next_page()
    print(f&quot;number of items we just fetched: {len(next_page.data)}&quot;)

    <span class="pl-s1">limit</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">20</span>,

    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">f"number of items we just fetched: <span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-en">len</span>(<span class="pl-s1">next_page</span>.<span class="pl-c1">data</span>)<span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>"</span>)

<p dir="auto">Or just work directly with the returned data:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="first_page = await client.fine_tuning.jobs.list(

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Nested params</h2><a id="user-content-nested-params" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Nested params" href="#nested-params"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Nested parameters are dictionaries, typed using <code>TypedDict</code>, for example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI

        }

<span class="pl-s1">response</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">responses</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(

            <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>,

)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">File uploads</h2><a id="user-content-file-uploads" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: File uploads" href="#file-uploads"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Request parameters that correspond to file uploads can be passed as <code>bytes</code>, or a <a href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/os.html#os.PathLike" rel="nofollow"><code>PathLike</code></a> instance or a tuple of <code>(filename, contents, media type)</code>.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from pathlib import Path
from openai import OpenAI

client = OpenAI()

client.files.create(
    file=Path(&quot;input.jsonl&quot;),
    purpose=&quot;fine-tune&quot;,
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">pathlib</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Path</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>()

<span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">files</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">Path</span>(<span class="pl-s">"input.jsonl"</span>),
    <span class="pl-s1">purpose</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"fine-tune"</span>,
)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The async client uses the exact same interface. If you pass a <a href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/os.html#os.PathLike" rel="nofollow"><code>PathLike</code></a> instance, the file contents will be read asynchronously automatically.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Webhook Verification</h2><a id="user-content-webhook-verification" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Webhook Verification" href="#webhook-verification"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Verifying webhook signatures is <em>optional but encouraged</em>.</p>
<p dir="auto">For more information about webhooks, see <a href="https://platform.openai.com/docs/guides/webhooks" rel="nofollow">the API docs</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Parsing webhook payloads</h3><a id="user-content-parsing-webhook-payloads" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Parsing webhook payloads" href="#parsing-webhook-payloads"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For most use cases, you will likely want to verify the webhook and parse the payload at the same time. To achieve this, we provide the method <code>client.webhooks.unwrap()</code>, which parses a webhook request and verifies that it was sent by OpenAI. This method will raise an error if the signature is invalid.</p>
<p dir="auto">Note that the <code>body</code> parameter must be the raw JSON string sent from the server (do not parse it first). The <code>.unwrap()</code> method will parse this JSON for you into an event object after verifying the webhook was sent from OpenAI.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI
from flask import Flask, request

app = Flask(__name__)
client = OpenAI()  # OPENAI_WEBHOOK_SECRET environment variable is used by default


@app.route(&quot;/webhook&quot;, methods=[&quot;POST&quot;])
def webhook():
    request_body = request.get_data(as_text=True)

    try:
        event = client.webhooks.unwrap(request_body, request.headers)

        if event.type == &quot;response.completed&quot;:
            print(&quot;Response completed:&quot;, event.data)
        elif event.type == &quot;response.failed&quot;:
            print(&quot;Response failed:&quot;, event.data)
        else:
            print(&quot;Unhandled event type:&quot;, event.type)

        return &quot;ok&quot;
    except Exception as e:
        print(&quot;Invalid signature:&quot;, e)
        return &quot;Invalid signature&quot;, 400


if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    app.run(port=8000)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">flask</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Flask</span>, <span class="pl-s1">request</span>

<span class="pl-s1">app</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Flask</span>(<span class="pl-s1">__name__</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>()  <span class="pl-c"># OPENAI_WEBHOOK_SECRET environment variable is used by default</span>


<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-s1">app</span>.<span class="pl-c1">route</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/webhook"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">methods</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[<span class="pl-s">"POST"</span>])</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">webhook</span>():
    <span class="pl-s1">request_body</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">request</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get_data</span>(<span class="pl-s1">as_text</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>)

    <span class="pl-k">try</span>:
        <span class="pl-s1">event</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">webhooks</span>.<span class="pl-c1">unwrap</span>(<span class="pl-s1">request_body</span>, <span class="pl-s1">request</span>.<span class="pl-c1">headers</span>)

        <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s">"response.completed"</span>:
            <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Response completed:"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">data</span>)
        <span class="pl-k">elif</span> <span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s">"response.failed"</span>:
            <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Response failed:"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">data</span>)
        <span class="pl-k">else</span>:
            <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Unhandled event type:"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">event</span>.<span class="pl-c1">type</span>)

        <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s">"ok"</span>
    <span class="pl-k">except</span> <span class="pl-v">Exception</span> <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">e</span>:
        <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Invalid signature:"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">e</span>)
        <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s">"Invalid signature"</span>, <span class="pl-c1">400</span>


<span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">__name__</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s">"__main__"</span>:
    <span class="pl-s1">app</span>.<span class="pl-c1">run</span>(<span class="pl-s1">port</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">8000</span>)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Verifying webhook payloads directly</h3><a id="user-content-verifying-webhook-payloads-directly" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Verifying webhook payloads directly" href="#verifying-webhook-payloads-directly"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">In some cases, you may want to verify the webhook separately from parsing the payload. If you prefer to handle these steps separately, we provide the method <code>client.webhooks.verify_signature()</code> to <em>only verify</em> the signature of a webhook request. Like <code>.unwrap()</code>, this method will raise an error if the signature is invalid.</p>
<p dir="auto">Note that the <code>body</code> parameter must be the raw JSON string sent from the server (do not parse it first). You will then need to parse the body after verifying the signature.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import json
from openai import OpenAI
from flask import Flask, request

app = Flask(__name__)
client = OpenAI()  # OPENAI_WEBHOOK_SECRET environment variable is used by default


@app.route(&quot;/webhook&quot;, methods=[&quot;POST&quot;])
def webhook():
    request_body = request.get_data(as_text=True)

    try:
        client.webhooks.verify_signature(request_body, request.headers)

        # Parse the body after verification
        event = json.loads(request_body)
        print(&quot;Verified event:&quot;, event)

        return &quot;ok&quot;
    except Exception as e:
        print(&quot;Invalid signature:&quot;, e)
        return &quot;Invalid signature&quot;, 400


if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    app.run(port=8000)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">json</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">flask</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Flask</span>, <span class="pl-s1">request</span>

<span class="pl-s1">app</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Flask</span>(<span class="pl-s1">__name__</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>()  <span class="pl-c"># OPENAI_WEBHOOK_SECRET environment variable is used by default</span>


<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-s1">app</span>.<span class="pl-c1">route</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/webhook"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">methods</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[<span class="pl-s">"POST"</span>])</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">webhook</span>():
    <span class="pl-s1">request_body</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">request</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get_data</span>(<span class="pl-s1">as_text</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>)

    <span class="pl-k">try</span>:
        <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">webhooks</span>.<span class="pl-c1">verify_signature</span>(<span class="pl-s1">request_body</span>, <span class="pl-s1">request</span>.<span class="pl-c1">headers</span>)

        <span class="pl-c"># Parse the body after verification</span>
        <span class="pl-s1">event</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">json</span>.<span class="pl-c1">loads</span>(<span class="pl-s1">request_body</span>)
        <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Verified event:"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">event</span>)

        <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s">"ok"</span>
    <span class="pl-k">except</span> <span class="pl-v">Exception</span> <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">e</span>:
        <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Invalid signature:"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">e</span>)
        <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s">"Invalid signature"</span>, <span class="pl-c1">400</span>


<span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">__name__</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s">"__main__"</span>:
    <span class="pl-s1">app</span>.<span class="pl-c1">run</span>(<span class="pl-s1">port</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">8000</span>)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Handling errors</h2><a id="user-content-handling-errors" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Handling errors" href="#handling-errors"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When the library is unable to connect to the API (for example, due to network connection problems or a timeout), a subclass of <code>openai.APIConnectionError</code> is raised.</p>
<p dir="auto">When the API returns a non-success status code (that is, 4xx or 5xx
response), a subclass of <code>openai.APIStatusError</code> is raised, containing <code>status_code</code> and <code>response</code> properties.</p>
<p dir="auto">All errors inherit from <code>openai.APIError</code>.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import openai
from openai import OpenAI

client = OpenAI()

try:
    client.fine_tuning.jobs.create(
        model=&quot;gpt-4o&quot;,
        training_file=&quot;file-abc123&quot;,
    )
except openai.APIConnectionError as e:
    print(&quot;The server could not be reached&quot;)
    print(e.__cause__)  # an underlying Exception, likely raised within HTTPX2.
except openai.RateLimitError as e:
    print(&quot;A 429 status code was received; we should back off a bit.&quot;)
except openai.APIStatusError as e:
    print(&quot;Another non-200-range status code was received&quot;)
    print(e.status_code)
    print(e.response)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>()

<span class="pl-k">try</span>:
    <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">fine_tuning</span>.<span class="pl-c1">jobs</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-4o"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">training_file</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"file-abc123"</span>,
    )
<span class="pl-k">except</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span>.<span class="pl-c1">APIConnectionError</span> <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">e</span>:
    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"The server could not be reached"</span>)
    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">e</span>.<span class="pl-c1">__cause__</span>)  <span class="pl-c"># an underlying Exception, likely raised within HTTPX2.</span>
<span class="pl-k">except</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span>.<span class="pl-c1">RateLimitError</span> <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">e</span>:
    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"A 429 status code was received; we should back off a bit."</span>)
<span class="pl-k">except</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span>.<span class="pl-c1">APIStatusError</span> <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">e</span>:
    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Another non-200-range status code was received"</span>)
    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">e</span>.<span class="pl-c1">status_code</span>)
    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">e</span>.<span class="pl-c1">response</span>)</pre></div>
