<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Request IDs</h2><a id="user-content-request-ids" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Request IDs" href="#request-ids"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">For more information on debugging requests, see <a href="https://platform.openai.com/docs/api-reference/debugging-requests" rel="nofollow">these docs</a></p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">All object responses in the SDK provide a <code>_request_id</code> property which is added from the <code>x-request-id</code> response header so that you can quickly log failing requests and report them back to OpenAI.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="response = await client.responses.create(
    model=&quot;gpt-5.5&quot;,
    input=&quot;Say 'this is a test'.&quot;,
)
print(response._request_id)  # req_123"><pre><span class="pl-s1">response</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">responses</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-5.5"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">input</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"Say 'this is a test'."</span>,
)
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">_request_id</span>)  <span class="pl-c"># req_123</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Note that unlike other properties that use an <code>_</code> prefix, the <code>_request_id</code> property
<em>is</em> public. Unless documented otherwise, <em>all</em> other <code>_</code> prefix properties,
methods and modules are <em>private</em>.</p>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-important" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-report mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 1.75C0 .784.784 0 1.75 0h12.5C15.216 0 16 .784 16 1.75v9.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.25 13H8.06l-2.573 2.573A1.458 1.458 0 0 1 3 14.543V13H1.75A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 0 11.25Zm1.75-.25a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v9.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h2a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.19l2.72-2.72a.749.749 0 0 1 .53-.22h6.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-9.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25-.25Zm7 2.25v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 9a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Important</p><p dir="auto">If you need to access request IDs for failed requests you must catch the <code>APIStatusError</code> exception</p>
</div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import openai

try:
    completion = await client.chat.completions.create(
        messages=[{&quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;, &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Say this is a test&quot;}], model=&quot;gpt-5.5&quot;
    )
except openai.APIStatusError as exc:
    print(exc.request_id)  # req_123
    raise exc"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span>

<span class="pl-k">try</span>:
    <span class="pl-s1">completion</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">chat</span>.<span class="pl-c1">completions</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">messages</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[{<span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Say this is a test"</span>}], <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-5.5"</span>
    )
<span class="pl-k">except</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span>.<span class="pl-c1">APIStatusError</span> <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">exc</span>:
    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">exc</span>.<span class="pl-c1">request_id</span>)  <span class="pl-c"># req_123</span>
    <span class="pl-k">raise</span> <span class="pl-s1">exc</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Retries</h2><a id="user-content-retries" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Retries" href="#retries"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Certain errors are automatically retried 2 times by default, with a short exponential backoff.
Connection errors (for example, due to a network connectivity problem), 408 Request Timeout, 409 Conflict,
429 Rate Limit, and &gt;=500 Internal errors are all retried by default.</p>
<p dir="auto">You can use the <code>max_retries</code> option to configure or disable retry settings:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI

# Configure the default for all requests:
client = OpenAI(
    # default is 2
    max_retries=0,
)

# Or, configure per-request:
client.with_options(max_retries=5).chat.completions.create(
    messages=[
        {
            &quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;,
            &quot;content&quot;: &quot;How can I get the name of the current day in JavaScript?&quot;,
        }
    ],
    model=&quot;gpt-5.5&quot;,
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-c"># Configure the default for all requests:</span>
<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-c"># default is 2</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">max_retries</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>,
)

<span class="pl-c"># Or, configure per-request:</span>
<span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">with_options</span>(<span class="pl-s1">max_retries</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">5</span>).<span class="pl-c1">chat</span>.<span class="pl-c1">completions</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">messages</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[
        {
            <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>,
            <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"How can I get the name of the current day in JavaScript?"</span>,
        }
    ],
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-5.5"</span>,
)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Timeouts</h2><a id="user-content-timeouts" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Timeouts" href="#timeouts"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">By default requests time out after 10 minutes. You can configure this with a <code>timeout</code> option,
which accepts a float or an <a href="https://httpx2.pydantic.dev/" rel="nofollow"><code>httpx2.Timeout</code></a> object:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import httpx2
from openai import OpenAI

# Configure the default for all requests:
client = OpenAI(
    # 20 seconds (default is 10 minutes)
    timeout=20.0,
)

# More granular control:
client = OpenAI(
    timeout=httpx2.Timeout(60.0, read=5.0, write=10.0, connect=2.0),
)

# Override per-request:
client.with_options(timeout=5.0).chat.completions.create(
    messages=[
        {
            &quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;,
            &quot;content&quot;: &quot;How can I list all files in a directory using Python?&quot;,
        }
    ],
    model=&quot;gpt-5.5&quot;,
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">httpx2</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-c"># Configure the default for all requests:</span>
<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-c"># 20 seconds (default is 10 minutes)</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">timeout</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">20.0</span>,
)

<span class="pl-c"># More granular control:</span>
<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">timeout</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">httpx2</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Timeout</span>(<span class="pl-c1">60.0</span>, <span class="pl-s1">read</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">5.0</span>, <span class="pl-s1">write</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">10.0</span>, <span class="pl-s1">connect</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">2.0</span>),
)

<span class="pl-c"># Override per-request:</span>
<span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">with_options</span>(<span class="pl-s1">timeout</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">5.0</span>).<span class="pl-c1">chat</span>.<span class="pl-c1">completions</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">messages</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[
        {
            <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>,
            <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"How can I list all files in a directory using Python?"</span>,
        }
    ],
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-5.5"</span>,
)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">On timeout, an <code>APITimeoutError</code> is thrown.</p>
<p dir="auto">Note that requests that time out are <a href="#retries">retried twice by default</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Advanced</h2><a id="user-content-advanced" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Advanced" href="#advanced"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Logging</h3><a id="user-content-logging" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Logging" href="#logging"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We use the standard library <a href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/logging.html" rel="nofollow"><code>logging</code></a> module.</p>
<p dir="auto">You can enable logging by setting the environment variable <code>OPENAI_LOG</code> to <code>info</code>.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$ export OPENAI_LOG=info"><pre>$ <span class="pl-k">export</span> OPENAI_LOG=info</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Or to <code>debug</code> for more verbose logging.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How to tell whether <code>None</code> means <code>null</code> or missing</h3><a id="user-content-how-to-tell-whether-none-means-null-or-missing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How to tell whether None means null or missing" href="#how-to-tell-whether-none-means-null-or-missing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">In an API response, a field may be explicitly <code>null</code>, or missing entirely; in either case, its value is <code>None</code> in this library. You can differentiate the two cases with <code>.model_fields_set</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="if response.my_field is None:
  if 'my_field' not in response.model_fields_set:
    print('Got json like {}, without a &quot;my_field&quot; key present at all.')
  else:
    print('Got json like {&quot;my_field&quot;: null}.')"><pre><span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">my_field</span> <span class="pl-c1">is</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span>:
  <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s">'my_field'</span> <span class="pl-c1"><span class="pl-c1">not</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span></span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">model_fields_set</span>:
    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">'Got json like {}, without a "my_field" key present at all.'</span>)
  <span class="pl-k">else</span>:
    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">'Got json like {"my_field": null}.'</span>)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Accessing raw response data (e.g. headers)</h3><a id="user-content-accessing-raw-response-data-eg-headers" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Accessing raw response data (e.g. headers)" href="#accessing-raw-response-data-eg-headers"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The "raw" Response object can be accessed by prefixing <code>.with_raw_response.</code> to any HTTP method call, e.g.,</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI

client = OpenAI()
response = client.chat.completions.with_raw_response.create(
    messages=[{
        &quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;,
        &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Say this is a test&quot;,
    }],
    model=&quot;gpt-5.5&quot;,
)
print(response.headers.get('X-My-Header'))

completion = response.parse()  # get the object that `chat.completions.create()` would have returned
print(completion)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>()
<span class="pl-s1">response</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">chat</span>.<span class="pl-c1">completions</span>.<span class="pl-c1">with_raw_response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">messages</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[{
        <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Say this is a test"</span>,
    }],
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-5.5"</span>,
)
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">headers</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">'X-My-Header'</span>))

<span class="pl-s1">completion</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">parse</span>()  <span class="pl-c"># get the object that `chat.completions.create()` would have returned</span>
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">completion</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">These methods return a <a href="https://github.com/openai/openai-python/tree/main/src/openai/_legacy_response.py"><code>LegacyAPIResponse</code></a> object. This is a legacy class as we're changing it slightly in the next major version.</p>
<p dir="auto">For the sync client this will mostly be the same with the exception
of <code>content</code> &amp; <code>text</code> will be methods instead of properties. In the
async client, all methods will be async.</p>
<p dir="auto">A migration script will be provided &amp; the migration in general should
be smooth.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>.with_streaming_response</code></h4><a id="user-content-with_streaming_response" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: .with_streaming_response" href="#with_streaming_response"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The above interface eagerly reads the full response body when you make the request, which may not always be what you want.</p>
<p dir="auto">To stream the response body, use <code>.with_streaming_response</code> instead, which requires a context manager and only reads the response body once you call <code>.read()</code>, <code>.text()</code>, <code>.json()</code>, <code>.iter_bytes()</code>, <code>.iter_text()</code>, <code>.iter_lines()</code> or <code>.parse()</code>. In the async client, these are async methods.</p>
<p dir="auto">As such, <code>.with_streaming_response</code> methods return a different <a href="https://github.com/openai/openai-python/tree/main/src/openai/_response.py"><code>APIResponse</code></a> object, and the async client returns an <a href="https://github.com/openai/openai-python/tree/main/src/openai/_response.py"><code>AsyncAPIResponse</code></a> object.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="with client.chat.completions.with_streaming_response.create(
    messages=[
        {
            &quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;,
            &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Say this is a test&quot;,
        }
    ],
    model=&quot;gpt-5.5&quot;,
) as response:
    print(response.headers.get(&quot;X-My-Header&quot;))

    for line in response.iter_lines():
        print(line)"><pre><span class="pl-k">with</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">chat</span>.<span class="pl-c1">completions</span>.<span class="pl-c1">with_streaming_response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">messages</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[
        {
            <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>,
            <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Say this is a test"</span>,
        }
    ],
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-5.5"</span>,
) <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span>:
    <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">headers</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"X-My-Header"</span>))

    <span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">line</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">iter_lines</span>():
        <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">line</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The context manager is required so that the response will reliably be closed.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Making custom/undocumented requests</h3><a id="user-content-making-customundocumented-requests" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Making custom/undocumented requests" href="#making-customundocumented-requests"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This library is typed for convenient access to the documented API.</p>
<p dir="auto">If you need to access undocumented endpoints, params, or response properties, the library can still be used.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Undocumented endpoints</h4><a id="user-content-undocumented-endpoints" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Undocumented endpoints" href="#undocumented-endpoints"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To make requests to undocumented endpoints, you can make requests using <code>client.get</code>, <code>client.post</code>, and other
http verbs. Options on the client will be respected (such as retries) when making this request.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import httpx2

response = client.post(
    &quot;/foo&quot;,
    cast_to=httpx2.Response,
    body={&quot;my_param&quot;: True},
)

print(response.headers.get(&quot;x-foo&quot;))"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">httpx2</span>

<span class="pl-s1">response</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">post</span>(
    <span class="pl-s">"/foo"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">cast_to</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">httpx2</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Response</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">body</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>{<span class="pl-s">"my_param"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">True</span>},
)

<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">headers</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x-foo"</span>))</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Undocumented request params</h4><a id="user-content-undocumented-request-params" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Undocumented request params" href="#undocumented-request-params"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you want to explicitly send an extra param, you can do so with the <code>extra_query</code>, <code>extra_body</code>, and <code>extra_headers</code> request
options.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Undocumented response properties</h4><a id="user-content-undocumented-response-properties" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Undocumented response properties" href="#undocumented-response-properties"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To access undocumented response properties, you can access the extra fields like <code>response.unknown_prop</code>. You
can also get all the extra fields on the Pydantic model as a dict with
<a href="https://docs.pydantic.dev/latest/api/base_model/#pydantic.BaseModel.model_extra" rel="nofollow"><code>response.model_extra</code></a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Configuring the HTTP client</h3><a id="user-content-configuring-the-http-client" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Configuring the HTTP client" href="#configuring-the-http-client"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can override the <a href="https://httpx2.pydantic.dev/" rel="nofollow">HTTPX2 client</a> to customize proxies, transports, authentication, event hooks, and other advanced HTTP behavior. See the <a href="/openai/openai-python/blob/main/httpx2.md">HTTPX2 migration guide</a> when updating an existing custom client.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import httpx2
from openai import OpenAI, DefaultHttpx2Client

client = OpenAI(
    # Or use the `OPENAI_BASE_URL` env var
    base_url=&quot;http://my.test.server.example.com:8083/v1&quot;,
    http_client=DefaultHttpx2Client(
        proxy=&quot;http://my.test.proxy.example.com&quot;,
        transport=httpx2.HTTPTransport(local_address=&quot;0.0.0.0&quot;),
    ),
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">httpx2</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>, <span class="pl-v">DefaultHttpx2Client</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-c"># Or use the `OPENAI_BASE_URL` env var</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">base_url</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"http://my.test.server.example.com:8083/v1"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">http_client</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">DefaultHttpx2Client</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">proxy</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"http://my.test.proxy.example.com"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">transport</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">httpx2</span>.<span class="pl-c1">HTTPTransport</span>(<span class="pl-s1">local_address</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"0.0.0.0"</span>),
    ),
)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You can also customize the client on a per-request basis by using <code>with_options()</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="client.with_options(http_client=DefaultHttpx2Client(...))"><pre><span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">with_options</span>(<span class="pl-s1">http_client</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">DefaultHttpx2Client</span>(...))</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Mutual TLS</h4><a id="user-content-mutual-tls" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Mutual TLS" href="#mutual-tls"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Before configuring a client, review the
<a href="https://help.openai.com/en/articles/10876024-openai-mutual-tls-beta-program" rel="nofollow">OpenAI Mutual TLS Beta Program</a>
for enrollment, currently supported endpoints, and certificate requirements.</p>
<p dir="auto">For API-key authenticated HTTP requests that require mutual TLS (mTLS), configure
a native <a href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/ssl.html#ssl.SSLContext" rel="nofollow"><code>ssl.SSLContext</code></a>
and pass it through the custom HTTP client:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import os
import ssl

from openai import OpenAI, DefaultHttpx2Client

# Server trust is configured independently. Without `cafile`, this uses the
# operating system's normal trusted certificate authorities.
ssl_context = ssl.create_default_context(
    cafile=os.environ.get(&quot;OPENAI_MTLS_CA_BUNDLE&quot;),
)
ssl_context.load_cert_chain(
    # This PEM must contain the leaf certificate first, followed by every
    # intermediate certificate needed to reach the server's trust anchor.
    certfile=os.environ[&quot;OPENAI_MTLS_CERTIFICATE_CHAIN&quot;],
    keyfile=os.environ[&quot;OPENAI_MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY&quot;],
    password=os.environ.get(&quot;OPENAI_MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD&quot;),
)

client = OpenAI(
    api_key=os.environ[&quot;OPENAI_API_KEY&quot;],
    # A custom HTTP client does not tell the SDK that mTLS is configured, so
    # select the mTLS endpoint explicitly. Preserve an EU or custom override.
    base_url=os.environ.get(
        &quot;OPENAI_BASE_URL&quot;,
        &quot;https://mtls.api.openai.com/v1&quot;,
    ),
    # A client certificate belongs to the HTTP client, not the base URL.
    # Disable redirects so it cannot follow a response to another origin.
    http_client=DefaultHttpx2Client(
        verify=ssl_context,
        follow_redirects=False,
    ),
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">os</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">ssl</span>

<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>, <span class="pl-v">DefaultHttpx2Client</span>

<span class="pl-c"># Server trust is configured independently. Without `cafile`, this uses the</span>
<span class="pl-c"># operating system's normal trusted certificate authorities.</span>
<span class="pl-s1">ssl_context</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">ssl</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create_default_context</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">cafile</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_MTLS_CA_BUNDLE"</span>),
)
<span class="pl-s1">ssl_context</span>.<span class="pl-c1">load_cert_chain</span>(
    <span class="pl-c"># This PEM must contain the leaf certificate first, followed by every</span>
    <span class="pl-c"># intermediate certificate needed to reach the server's trust anchor.</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">certfile</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>[<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_MTLS_CERTIFICATE_CHAIN"</span>],
    <span class="pl-s1">keyfile</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>[<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY"</span>],
    <span class="pl-s1">password</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD"</span>),
)

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">api_key</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>[<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_API_KEY"</span>],
    <span class="pl-c"># A custom HTTP client does not tell the SDK that mTLS is configured, so</span>
    <span class="pl-c"># select the mTLS endpoint explicitly. Preserve an EU or custom override.</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">base_url</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(
        <span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_BASE_URL"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s">"https://mtls.api.openai.com/v1"</span>,
    ),
    <span class="pl-c"># A client certificate belongs to the HTTP client, not the base URL.</span>
    <span class="pl-c"># Disable redirects so it cannot follow a response to another origin.</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">http_client</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">DefaultHttpx2Client</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">verify</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">ssl_context</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">follow_redirects</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">False</span>,
    ),
)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The async configuration is equivalent:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import os
import ssl

from openai import AsyncOpenAI, DefaultAsyncHttpx2Client

ssl_context = ssl.create_default_context(
    cafile=os.environ.get(&quot;OPENAI_MTLS_CA_BUNDLE&quot;),
)
ssl_context.load_cert_chain(
    certfile=os.environ[&quot;OPENAI_MTLS_CERTIFICATE_CHAIN&quot;],
    keyfile=os.environ[&quot;OPENAI_MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY&quot;],
    password=os.environ.get(&quot;OPENAI_MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD&quot;),
)

client = AsyncOpenAI(
    api_key=os.environ[&quot;OPENAI_API_KEY&quot;],
    base_url=os.environ.get(
        &quot;OPENAI_BASE_URL&quot;,
        &quot;https://mtls.api.openai.com/v1&quot;,
    ),
    http_client=DefaultAsyncHttpx2Client(
        verify=ssl_context,
        follow_redirects=False,
    ),
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">os</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">ssl</span>

<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">AsyncOpenAI</span>, <span class="pl-v">DefaultAsyncHttpx2Client</span>

<span class="pl-s1">ssl_context</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">ssl</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create_default_context</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">cafile</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_MTLS_CA_BUNDLE"</span>),
)
<span class="pl-s1">ssl_context</span>.<span class="pl-c1">load_cert_chain</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">certfile</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>[<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_MTLS_CERTIFICATE_CHAIN"</span>],
    <span class="pl-s1">keyfile</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>[<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY"</span>],
    <span class="pl-s1">password</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD"</span>),
)

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">AsyncOpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">api_key</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>[<span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_API_KEY"</span>],
    <span class="pl-s1">base_url</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">environ</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(
        <span class="pl-s">"OPENAI_BASE_URL"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s">"https://mtls.api.openai.com/v1"</span>,
    ),
    <span class="pl-s1">http_client</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">DefaultAsyncHttpx2Client</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">verify</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">ssl_context</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">follow_redirects</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">False</span>,
    ),
)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">See the complete <a href="/openai/openai-python/blob/main/examples/mtls_httpx2.py">sync HTTPX2</a> and
<a href="/openai/openai-python/blob/main/examples/mtls_httpx2_async.py">async HTTPX2</a> examples.</p>
<p dir="auto">The certificate-bearing HTTP client is transport-wide. For API-key mTLS,
dedicate it to the selected API origin; X.509 workload identity also uses the
fixed OpenAI mTLS token-exchange origin. Do not reuse the client for unrelated
services or pass it through <code>with_options()</code> with a different <code>base_url</code>.
If redirects are required for API-key mTLS, add an
HTTPX2 request hook that rejects requests whose scheme, host, or port differs
from the configured mTLS origin before enabling <code>follow_redirects</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>SSLContext.load_cert_chain()</code> raises during setup for unreadable or malformed
files and for a private key that does not match the leaf certificate. Certificate
expiry, key usage, extended key usage, SAN, and trust policy remain TLS server
decisions. OpenAI does not fetch missing intermediates through AIA, so provide a
complete, leaf-first client-chain PEM. Intermediate-chain support is currently
enabled by request. Until it is enabled for your organization, use a client leaf
certificate directly signed by the uploaded CA.</p>
<p dir="auto">For certificate rotation, build a new <code>SSLContext</code>, HTTP client, and <code>OpenAI</code> or
<code>AsyncOpenAI</code> client. This creates a fresh connection pool; close the old SDK
client after its in-flight requests finish. Do not assume existing TLS
connections will renegotiate.</p>
<p dir="auto">This recipe applies to ordinary API-key HTTP traffic. For certificate-backed
token exchange, use the X.509 workload identity configuration described above.
Realtime WebSocket mTLS is not included.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Managing HTTP resources</h3><a id="user-content-managing-http-resources" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Managing HTTP resources" href="#managing-http-resources"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">By default the library closes underlying HTTP connections whenever the client is <a href="https://docs.python.org/3/reference/datamodel.html#object.__del__" rel="nofollow">garbage collected</a>. You can manually close the client using the <code>.close()</code> method if desired, or with a context manager that closes when exiting.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI

with OpenAI() as client:
  # make requests here
  ...

# HTTP client is now closed"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-k">with</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>() <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>:
  <span class="pl-c"># make requests here</span>
  ...

<span class="pl-c"># HTTP client is now closed</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Microsoft Azure OpenAI</h2><a id="user-content-microsoft-azure-openai" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Microsoft Azure OpenAI" href="#microsoft-azure-openai"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To use this library with <a href="https://learn.microsoft.com/azure/ai-services/openai/overview" rel="nofollow">Azure OpenAI</a>, use the <code>AzureOpenAI</code>
class instead of the <code>OpenAI</code> class.</p>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-important" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-report mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 1.75C0 .784.784 0 1.75 0h12.5C15.216 0 16 .784 16 1.75v9.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.25 13H8.06l-2.573 2.573A1.458 1.458 0 0 1 3 14.543V13H1.75A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 0 11.25Zm1.75-.25a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v9.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h2a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.19l2.72-2.72a.749.749 0 0 1 .53-.22h6.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-9.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25-.25Zm7 2.25v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 9a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Important</p><p dir="auto">The Azure API shape differs from the core API shape which means that the static types for responses / params
won't always be correct.</p>
</div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import AzureOpenAI

<p dir="auto">In addition to the options provided in the base <code>OpenAI</code> client, the following options are provided:</p>
<ul dir="auto">

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Amazon Bedrock</h2><a id="user-content-amazon-bedrock" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Amazon Bedrock" href="#amazon-bedrock"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To use this library with <a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/models-api-compatibility.html" rel="nofollow">Amazon Bedrock's OpenAI-compatible API</a>, configure the standard <code>OpenAI</code> client with the Bedrock provider.</p>
<p dir="auto">Install the optional Bedrock dependencies to use the standard AWS credential chain and SigV4 authentication:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install 'openai[bedrock]'"><pre>pip install <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>openai[bedrock]<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI
from openai.providers import bedrock

# Uses your normal AWS credentials. You can omit region when it is
# configured through AWS_REGION, AWS_DEFAULT_REGION, or your AWS profile.
client = OpenAI(
    provider=bedrock(
        region=&quot;us-west-2&quot;,
    )
)

response = client.responses.create(
    model=&quot;openai.gpt-5.4&quot;,
    input=&quot;Say hello!&quot;,
)

print(response.output_text)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span>.<span class="pl-s1">providers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">bedrock</span>

<span class="pl-c"># Uses your normal AWS credentials. You can omit region when it is</span>
<span class="pl-c"># configured through AWS_REGION, AWS_DEFAULT_REGION, or your AWS profile.</span>
<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">provider</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">bedrock</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">region</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"us-west-2"</span>,
    )
)

<span class="pl-s1">response</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">responses</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"openai.gpt-5.4"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">input</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"Say hello!"</span>,
)

<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">output_text</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The provider configures AWS authentication and the Bedrock Mantle endpoint while retaining the normal SDK resources, retries, streaming, and error handling. AWS controls which endpoints and features are supported; unsupported calls surface the provider's normal HTTP errors through the SDK.</p>
<p dir="auto">The default AWS credential chain supports environment credentials, shared credentials and config files, named profiles, SSO and assume-role profiles, and workload credentials such as ECS, EKS, and EC2 metadata. To select a named profile:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="client = OpenAI(
    provider=bedrock(
        profile=&quot;my-profile&quot;,
    )
)"><pre><span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">provider</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">bedrock</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">profile</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"my-profile"</span>,
    )
)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You can also pass <code>access_key_id</code> and <code>secret_access_key</code>, with an optional <code>session_token</code>, or a refreshable <code>credential_provider</code> that returns botocore-compatible credentials. Explicit bearer and AWS credential options are mutually exclusive.</p>
<p dir="auto">Pass <code>base_url</code> to <code>bedrock(...)</code> or set <code>AWS_BEDROCK_BASE_URL</code> to override the derived <code>https://bedrock-mantle.&lt;region&gt;.api.aws/openai/v1</code> endpoint. Custom URLs retain Mantle signing by default; pass <code>endpoint="runtime"</code> to use Runtime signing.</p>
<p dir="auto">SigV4 requests require replayable, fully serialized request bodies. Standard JSON requests already meet this requirement, and response streaming is unaffected. Low-level one-shot request streams must be buffered before sending, or sent with bearer authentication and retries disabled.</p>
<p dir="auto">Bearer tokens remain available as a compatibility or manual authentication mode. Set <code>AWS_BEARER_TOKEN_BEDROCK</code> to an <a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/api-keys.html" rel="nofollow">Amazon Bedrock API key</a>, pass <code>api_key</code>, or provide a refresh callback:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="client = OpenAI(
    provider=bedrock(
        region=&quot;us-west-2&quot;,
        token_provider=lambda: refresh_bedrock_token(),
    )
)"><pre><span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">provider</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">bedrock</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">region</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"us-west-2"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">token_provider</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-k">lambda</span>: <span class="pl-en">refresh_bedrock_token</span>(),
    )
)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Without explicit authentication, <code>AWS_BEARER_TOKEN_BEDROCK</code> takes precedence over the default AWS credential chain for backwards compatibility.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Legacy <code>BedrockOpenAI</code> client</h3><a id="user-content-legacy-bedrockopenai-client" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Legacy BedrockOpenAI client" href="#legacy-bedrockopenai-client"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>BedrockOpenAI</code> and <code>AsyncBedrockOpenAI</code> remain available for existing applications and delegate to the same provider implementation. New applications should prefer <code>OpenAI(provider=bedrock(...))</code>.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import BedrockOpenAI

client = BedrockOpenAI(
    aws_region=&quot;us-west-2&quot;,
    aws_profile=&quot;my-profile&quot;,
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">BedrockOpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">BedrockOpenAI</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">aws_region</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"us-west-2"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">aws_profile</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"my-profile"</span>,
)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The legacy module client also continues to support <code>openai.api_type = "amazon-bedrock"</code> or <code>OPENAI_API_TYPE=amazon-bedrock</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Versioning</h2><a id="user-content-versioning" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Versioning" href="#versioning"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This package generally follows <a href="https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html" rel="nofollow">SemVer</a> conventions, though certain backwards-incompatible changes may be released as minor versions:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Changes that only affect static types, without breaking runtime behavior.</li>
<li>Changes to library internals which are technically public but not intended or documented for external use. <em>(Please open a GitHub issue to let us know if you are relying on such internals.)</em></li>
<li>Changes that we do not expect to impact the vast majority of users in practice.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Minimum supported Python version increases are released as minor versions, not patches, when package metadata can keep users on the final compatible SDK release. See the <a href="/openai/openai-python/blob/main/PYTHON_VERSION_POLICY.md">Python version support policy</a> for the support window, release treatment, and compatibility history.</p>
<p dir="auto">We take backwards-compatibility seriously and work hard to ensure you can rely on a smooth upgrade experience.</p>
<p dir="auto">We are keen for your feedback; please open an <a href="https://www.github.com/openai/openai-python/issues">issue</a> with questions, bugs, or suggestions.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Determining the installed version</h3><a id="user-content-determining-the-installed-version" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Determining the installed version" href="#determining-the-installed-version"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you've upgraded to the latest version but aren't seeing any new features you were expecting then your python environment is likely still using an older version.</p>
<p dir="auto">You can determine the version that is being used at runtime with:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import openai
print(openai.__version__)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span>
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">openai</span>.<span class="pl-c1">__version__</span>)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Requirements</h2><a id="user-content-requirements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Requirements" href="#requirements"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Python 3.10 or higher.</p>
